compressImage(file) { return new Promise((resolve, reject) => { const reader = new FileReader(); reader.onload = ev => { const img = new Image(); img.onload = () => { const MAX = 800; let w = img.width, h = img.height; if (w > MAX || h > MAX) { if (w > h) { h = Math.round(h * MAX / w); w = MAX; } else { w = Math.round(w * MAX / h); h = MAX; } } const canvas = document.createElement("canvas"); canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h; canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h); resolve(canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.65)); }; img.onerror = reject; img.src = ev.target.result; }; reader.onerror = reject; reader.readAsDataURL(file); }); } // Upload compressed photo, return download URL async function uploadPhoto(section, recordId, file) { const storage = getStorage(); if (!storage) throw new Error("Storage not available"); const blob = await compressImage(file); const path = `farms/${FARM_ID}/${section}/${recordId}.jpg`; const ref = window.fbRef(storage, path); await window.fbUploadBytes(ref, blob, { contentType:"image/jpeg" }); return await window.fbGetDownloadURL(ref); } // Delete photo from Storage async function deletePhoto(section, recordId) { const storage = getStorage(); if (!storage) return; try { const ref = window.fbRef(storage, `farms/${FARM_ID}/${section}/${recordId}.jpg`); await } function Pagination({ page, setPage, totalPages, totalItems, perPage }) { if (totalPages <= 1) return null; const from = (page-1)*perPage + 1; const to = Math.min(page*perPage, totalItems); const pageNumbers = () => { const nums = []; }); } // ── PhotoLightbox: full-screen tap-to-zoom viewer with long-press-to-save ── function PhotoLightbox({ photo, onClose }) { const [scale, setScale] = useState(1); const [offset, setOffset] = useState({ x:0, y:0 }); setScale(s => Math.min(6, Math.max(1, s * ratio))); } lastTouchDist.current = dist; } else if (e.touches.length === 1 && scale > 1) { // Pan when zoomed in const dx = e.touches[0].clientX - (lastPanPos.current?.x || 0); } }; const onTouchEnd = e => { lastTouchDist.current = null; // Double-tap to reset zoom if (!isDragging.current && scale > 1) { setScale(1); setOffset({x:0,y:0}); } isDragging.current = false; }; // Close only if tapped background (not image) and not dragging const onBgClick = e => { if (e.target === e.currentTarget) onClose(); function RosterTab({ workers, attendance, setAttendance, deleteAttendanceMonth, appUsers=[] }) { // Local date (Malaysia UTC+8 safe) const todayLocal = (() => { const d=new Date(); return [d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0"),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")].join("-"); })(); const [rosterMonth, setRosterMonth] = useState(todayLocal.slice(0,7)); const [rosterPg, setRosterPg] = useState(1); const [markDate, setMarkDate] = useState(todayLocal); const [showMarkDay, setShowMarkDay] = useState(false); const [editMode, setEditMode] = useState(false); const ROSTER_PER = 8; const STATUSES = [ { id:"present", icon:"✓", label:"Present", col:"#2e7d46" }, { id:"halfday", icon:"½", label:"Half", col:"#b06000" }, { id:"sick", icon:"🤒", label:"Sick", col:"#2471a3" }, { id:"leave", icon:"🏖", label:"Leave", col:"#7c6fae" }, { id:"absent", icon:"✗", label:"Absent", col:"#c0392b" }, ]; const markStatus = (wid, status) => { const prev = attendance[markDate] || { records:{} }; const cur = (prev.records||{})[wid]; const newRec = { ...(prev.records||{}) }; if (cur===status) delete newRec[wid]; else newRec[wid]=status; setAttendance(a=>({ ...a, [markDate]:{ ...prev, records:newRec } })); }; const resetMonthAttendance = async () => { const ownerPin = (appUsers||[]).find(u=>u.role==="Owner")?.pin||"1234"; const pin = prompt("Enter Owner PIN to reset this month's roster:"); if (pin !== ownerPin) { if (pin !== null) alert("Wrong PIN"); return; } if (deleteAttendanceMonth) { await deleteAttendanceMonth(rosterMonth); alert(`✅ ${rosterMonth} roster cleared.`); } }; const [rosterYM, rosterY] = [rosterMonth, parseInt(rosterMonth.slice(0,4))]; const rosterMo = parseInt(rosterMonth.slice(5,7)) - 1; // 0-indexed const daysInMonth = new Date(rosterY, rosterMo + 1, 0).getDate(); const days = Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }, (_, i) => i + 1); const dayLabel = d => { const dt = new Date(rosterY, rosterMo, d); return ['Su','Mo','Tu','We','Th','Fr','Sa'][dt.getDay()]; }; const isWeekend = d => { const day = new Date(rosterY, rosterMo, d).getDay(); return day===0||day===6; }; const STATUS_MAP = { present: { short:'P', color:'#fff', bg:'#2e7d32', border:'#1b5e20' }, halfday: { short:'½', color:'#fff', bg:'#f57c00', border:'#e65100' }, sick: { short:'S', color:'#fff', bg:'#0277bd', border:'#01579b' }, leave: { short:'L', color:'#fff', bg:'#6a1b9a', border:'#4a148c' }, absent: { short:'A', color:'#fff', bg:'#c62828', border:'#b71c1c' }, }; const STATUS_CYCLE = [null,'present','halfday','sick','leave','absent']; const dateKey = (d) => `${rosterMonth}-${String(d).padStart(2,'0')}`; const getStatus = (wid, d) => { const rec = attendance[dateKey(d)]; return rec ? rec.records[wid] || null : null; }; const cycleStatus = (wid, d) => { if (!editMode) return; // locked const cur = getStatus(wid, d); const idx = STATUS_CYCLE.indexOf(cur); const next = STATUS_CYCLE[(idx+1) % STATUS_CYCLE.length]; const key = dateKey(d); const existing = attendance[key] || { weather:'sunny', records:{} }; const newRec = next === null ? (() => { const r = {...existing.records}; delete r[wid]; return {...existing, records:r}; })() : {...existing, records:{...existing.records, [wid]:next}}; setAttendance(a => ({...a, [key]:newRec})); }; // Per-worker summary const workerSummary = (wid) => { const counts = { present:0, halfday:0, sick:0, leave:0, absent:0 }; days.forEach(d => { const s = getStatus(wid, d); if (s && counts[s] !== undefined) counts[s]++; }); const workDays = counts.present + counts.halfday * 0.5; return { ...counts, workDays }; }; // Daily summary (headcount per day) const dayPresent = (d) => workers.filter(w => getStatus(w.id,d)==='present').length; const dayWorking = (d) => workers.filter(w => { const s = getStatus(w.id,d); return s==='present'||s==='halfday'; }).length; // ── Export roster to real .xlsx — custom wide-table format ── const exportRoster = () => { const monthName = new Date(rosterY, rosterMo, 1).toLocaleDateString('en-MY',{month:'long',year:'numeric'}); // STATUS colours for SpreadsheetML background fills (ARGB) const STATUS_XLSX = { present: { bg:'FF2E7D32', fg:'FFFFFFFF' }, halfday: { bg:'FFF57C00', fg:'FFFFFFFF' }, sick: { bg:'FF0277BD', fg:'FFFFFFFF' }, leave: { bg:'FF6A1B9A', fg:'FFFFFFFF' }, absent: { bg:'FFC62828', fg:'FFFFFFFF' }, }; // XML helpers const xe = v => String(v==null?'':v).replace(/&/g,'&').replace(//g,'>').replace(/"/g,'"'); const colLetter = n => { let s=''; n=n+1; while(n>0){const r=(n-1)%26; s=String.fromCharCode(65+r)+s; n=Math.floor((n-1)/26);} return s; }; const numCell = (col,row,val,sId) => { const ref = colLetter(col)+(row+1); const s = sId ? ` s="${sId}"` : ''; return Number.isFinite(Number(val)) && val!=='' ? `${Number(val)}` : `${xe(val)}`; }; // Build styles — we need one fill per status + standard styles const fills = [ '', '', // 2=header dark green, 3=weekend header, 4=summary col header, 5=footer '', '', '', '', // 6=present,7=halfday,8=sick,9=leave,10=absent '', '', '', '', '', // 11=odd row, 12=even row '', '', ]; // cellXfs indices: // 0=default, 1=title(bold big green), 2=subtitle(italic), 3=hdr-worker(white on dark), // 4=hdr-day(white on dark), 5=hdr-weekend(dark on light), 6=hdr-summary(colour on grey), // 7=body-name, 8=body-num, 9=body-status-base (overridden per status below), // 10=present-cell, 11=halfday-cell, 12=sick-cell, 13=leave-cell, 14=absent-cell, // 15=empty-cell, 16=footer-label, 17=footer-num, 18=total-label, 19=total-num const cellXfs = ` `; const statusStyleMap = { present:9, halfday:10, sick:11, leave:12, absent:13, '':14 }; const NCOLS = 2 + days.length + 6; // Name + Role + days + P+½+S+L+A+Days // Build rows let rowXml = []; let r = 0; // Row 1: Title rowXml.push(`Monthly Attendance Roster — ${xe(monthName)}`); r=1; // Row 2: Subtitle rowXml.push(`The Durian Farm · ${workers.length} workers · Exported ${new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-MY')}`); r=2; // Row 3: blank rowXml.push(``); r=3; // Row 4: Header — day of week { let cells = ''; cells += `Worker`; cells += `Role`; days.forEach((d,i) => { const sId = isWeekend(d) ? 5 : 4; cells += `${d}`; }); // Empty summary header row 2 for(let i=0; i<6; i++) cells += ``; rowXml.push(`${cells}`); r=5; } // Data rows: one per worker workers.forEach((w, wi) => { const sm = workerSummary(w.id); const rowNum = wi + 6; let cells = ''; cells += `${xe(w.name)}`; cells += `${xe(w.role)}`; days.forEach((d,i) => { const s = getStatus(w.id, d); const sId = s ? ); } // ── PayrollCard ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── // ── PayrollLedger: scrollable table, filter, PIN-edit, export ───────────── function PayrollLedger({ workers, advances, payouts, appUsers, calcStats, setWorkers, setPayouts, setAdvances, plantation, attendance={}, monthPresent }) { const [lSearch, setLSearch] = useState(""); const [lWorker, setLWorker] = useState("all"); const [lStatus, setLStatus] = useState("all"); const [lMonth, setLMonth] = useState("all"); const [lPage, setLPage] = useState(1); const [editCell, setEditCell] = useState(null); const [editVal, setEditVal] = useState(""); const [editPin, setEditPin] = useState(""); const [editPinErr,setEditPinErr] = useState(""); const LPER = 12; const ownerPin = (appUsers||[]).find(u=>u.role==="Owner")?.pin||"1234"; // payoutMonth: use p.month (work month) as canonical key — matches attendance data. // paidDate is only relevant in the Transactions tab for payment-date filtering. const payoutMonth = p => (p.month || p.paidDate || "").slice(0,7); // Build all months that have any data const allMonths = [...new Set([ ...advances.map(a=>a.date?.slice(0,7)).filter(Boolean), ...payouts.map(p=>payoutMonth(p)).filter(Boolean), // Also include months with attendance records for workers ...Object.keys(attendance||{}).map(d=>d.slice(0,7)), ])].sort().reverse(); // Helper: compute wages for a specific worker in a specific month // Each month is INDEPENDENT — no carryover const N = (v,f=0) => { const n=parseFloat(v); return isNaN(n)?f:n; }; const monthWages = (w, ym) => { let basePay; if (w.payType==="daily") { // Count actual days worked this month from attendance const attDays = monthPresent ? monthPresent(w.id, ym) : 0; basePay = N(w.dailyRate) * attDays; } else { basePay = N(w.baseSalary); } // Incentive: count Grade A fruits harvested by this worker this month // (simplified: use 0 for ledger — detailed incentive is in PayrollCard) return basePay; }; // Flatten to one row per worker × month — INDEPENDENT per month const allRows = []; workers.forEach(w => { allMonths.forEach(m => { const mAdv = advances.filter(a=>a.workerId===w.id && a.date?.startsWith(m)); const mPay = payouts.filter(p=>p.workerId===w.id && payoutMonth(p)===m); // Include month only if it has attendance, advance, or payout data const attDays = monthPresent ? const payTot = mPay.reduce((s,p)=>s+Number(p.netPaid||0),0); // Balance = wages still owed to worker after all advances + payouts const totalPaid = advTot + payTot; const balance = wages - totalPaid; // positive = still owed to worker allRows.push({ key: w.id+m, wid: w.id, name: w.name, role: w.role, month: m, monthLabel: new Date(m+"-02").toLocaleDateString("en-MY",{month:"short",year:"2-digit"}), wages, advTot, payTot, totalPaid, balance, attDays, unpaid: balance > 0, // owed to worker overpaid: balance < 0, // overpaid to worker settled: balance === 0, advances: mAdv, payments: mPay, }); }); }); // Filter const filtered = allRows.filter(r => { if (lWorker !== "all" && r.wid !== lWorker) return false; if (lMonth !== "all" && r.month !== lMonth) return false; if (lStatus === "overpaid" && !r.overpaid) return false; } return true; }); const totalPages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / LPER)); const pageRows = filtered.slice((lPage-1)*LPER, lPage*LPER); // Footer totals const totWages = filtered.reduce((s,r)=>s+r.wages,0); } const r = allRows.find(x=>x.key===editCell.rowKey); if (r) { if (editCell.field === "wages") { setWorkers(ws=>ws.map(w=>w.id===r.wid ? {...w, baseSalary:parseFloat(editVal)||w.baseSalary} : w)); } else if (editCell.field === "paid") { const p = payouts.find(x=>x.workerId===r.wid && x.month===r.month); setAdvances(a=>[...a, adv]); setAdvForm({ workerId:"", amount:"", reason:"", date:todayStr }); setAddingAdv(false); }; const savePayout = (wid, gross, advTotal, customDate, customAmt, customRef, photos=[]) => { const w = workers.find(w=>w.id===wid); const net = customAmt != null ? Number(customAmt) : Math.max(0, gross - advTotal); const date = customDate || todayStr; const ref = customRef || payoutForm.ref || ""; const po = { id:`PO${Date.now()}`, workerId:wid, workerName:w?.name||"", month:curMonth, gross, advancesDeducted:advTotal, netPaid:net, paidDate:date, ref, photos:photos||[] }; setPayouts(p=>[...p, po]); }; const { page, setPage: setPgLocal, totalPages, pageItems } = usePagination(workers, 6); const getDay = d => attendance[d] || { weather:"sunny", records:{} }; const setDay = (d, val) => setAttendance(a => ({ ...a, [d]:val })); const setWx = (d, wx) => setDay(d, { ...getDay(d), weather:wx }); const markAtt = (d, wid, status) => { setAttendance(a => { const day = a[d] || { weather:"sunny", records:{} }; return { ...a, [d]:{ ...day, records:{ ...day.records, [wid]:status } } }; }); }; // Pre-compute effective work days per worker per month: // present = 1 full day // halfday = 0.5 day // sick = 0 (no pay for daily workers) // leave = 0 (no pay for daily workers) // absent = 0 const presenceCache = useMemo(() => { const cache = {}; Object.entries(attendance||{}).forEach(([d, day]) => { const ym = d.slice(0,7); Object.entries(day.records||{}).forEach(([wid, status]) => { const key = wid+"_"+ym; if (status === "present") cache[key] = (cache[key]||0) + 1; if (status === "halfday") cache[key] = (cache[key]||0) + 0.5; // sick / leave / absent contribute 0 working days }); }); return cache; }, [attendance]); // Returns effective working days (float, e.g. 22.5) for the month const monthPresent = (wid, ym) => presenceCache[wid+"_"+ym] || 0; const setWorkersSync = fn => { if (setWorkersOuter) setWorkersOuter(fn); }; const updateWorker = (id, field, val) => setWorkersSync(ws => ws.map(w => w.id===id ? { ...w, [field]: val } : w)); const saveNew = () => { if (!newW.name.trim()) return; setWorkersSync(ws => [...ws, { ...newW, id:`W${Date.now()}` }]); setNewW(EMPTY_WORKER); setAdding(false); }; const deleteWorker = id => { setWorkersSync(ws => ws.filter(w => w.id!==id)); setSelected(null); setConfirmDel(null); }; const calcStats = w => { // Always parse to Number — worker fields may be stored as strings from form inputs const N = (v,fallback=0) => { const n=parseFloat(v); return isNaN(n)?fallback:n; }; const fruits = (harvest||[]).filter(h => h.worker === w.name.split(" ")[0]); const gradeA = fruits.filter(f => f.grade==="A").length; const rateA = N(plantation?.incentiveGradeA, 3); const rateF = N(plantation?.incentivePerFruit, 0.5); const incentive = Math.floor(gradeA * rateA + fruits.length * rateF); const curMonth = effectiveAttDate.slice(0,7); const loggedDays = monthPresent(w.id, curMonth); // Daily pay = logged attendance days only (present=1, halfday=0.5, sick/leave/absent=0) // No preset day assumption — pay is always based on actual attendance const basePay = w.payType==="daily" ? N(w.dailyRate) * loggedDays : N(w.baseSalary); const epfAmt = Math.round(basePay * (N(w.epfRate)/100)); const socsoAmt = Math.round(basePay * (N(w.socsoRate)/100)); const permitMo = Math.round(N(w.permitCostYear)/12); const medicalMo = Math.round(N(w.medicalYear)/12); const otherMo = N(w.otherMonth); const isMgmt = MGMT_ROLES.includes(w.role); const carAllow = isMgmt ? N(w.carAllowance) : 0; const phoneAllow = isMgmt ? N(w.phoneAllowance) : 0; const houseAllow = isMgmt ? N(w.housingAllowance): 0; const perfBonus = isMgmt ? Math.round(N(w.performanceBonus)/12) : 0; const totalCost = basePay + incentive + epfAmt + socsoAmt + permitMo + medicalMo + otherMo + carAllow + phoneAllow + houseAllow + perfBonus; return { ...w, fruits:fruits.length, gradeA, incentive, basePay, epfAmt, socsoAmt, permitMo, medicalMo, otherMo, carAllow, phoneAllow, houseAllow, perfBonus, totalCost }; }; // Month-aware version — used by WorkerFilteredList when viewMonth differs from roster month const calcStatsForMonth = (w, ym) => { const N = (v,fb=0) => { const n=parseFloat(v); return isNaN(n)?fb:n; }; const fruits = (harvest||[]).filter(h => h.worker === w.name.split(" ")[0]); const gradeA = fruits.filter(f => f.grade==="A").length; const rateA = N(plantation?.incentiveGradeA, 3); const rateF = N(plantation?.incentivePerFruit, 0.5); const incentive = Math.floor(gradeA * rateA + fruits.length * rateF); const loggedDays = monthPresent ? monthPresent(w.id, ym) : 0; const basePay = w.payType==="daily" ? Math.round(N(w.performanceBonus)/12) : 0; const totalCost = basePay + incentive + epfAmt + socsoAmt + permitMo + medicalMo + otherMo + carAllow + phoneAllow + houseAllow + perfBonus; return { ...w, fruits:fruits.length, gradeA, incentive, basePay, epfAmt, socsoAmt, permitMo, medicalMo, otherMo, carAllow, phoneAllow, houseAllow, perfBonus, totalCost }; }; const allStats = (workers||[]).map(calcStats); const pageStats = (pageItems||[]).map(calcStats); const grandTotal = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.totalCost,0); const tWages = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.basePay+b.incentive,0); const tEPF = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.epfAmt,0); const tSocso = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.socsoAmt,0); const tPermit = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.permitMo,0); const tMed = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.medicalMo,0); const tOther = allStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.otherMo,0); // Category split const staffWorkers = workers.filter(w=>(w.category||defaultCategory(w.role))==="staff"); const fieldWorkers = workers.filter(w=>(w.category||defaultCategory(w.role))==="worker"); const staffStats = staffWorkers.map(calcStats); const fieldStats = fieldWorkers.map(calcStats); const staffTotal = staffStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.totalCost,0); const fieldTotal = fieldStats.reduce((a,b)=>a+b.totalCost,0); return (

// ── Historical records const histRecords = plantation?.historicalWorkerCost||[]; const histTotal = histRecords.reduce((s,r)=>s+Number(r.amount||0),0); // ── Current payroll: filter by month/year/name const filtPayouts = payouts.filter(p => { const ym = p.month||""; // ── Excel export engine — pure client-side, no external libraries or network calls ── // Generates a REAL .xlsx file (Office Open XML / ZIP container) so it opens correctly // in desktop Excel, Google Sheets, LibreOffice, AND iOS/iPadOS Numbers. // Implemented from scratch: a minimal ZIP writer (store method, no compression needed) // plus the required XLSX XML parts, packed with Blob + URL.createObjectURL. function xlsxCrc32(bytes) { let crc = 0xFFFFFFFF; for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i++) { crc ^= bytes[i]; for (let j = 0; j < 8; j++) { crc = (crc >>> 1) ^ (0xEDB88320 & -(crc & 1)); } } return (crc ^ 0xFFFFFFFF) >>> 0; } function xlsxStrToBytes(str) { return new TextEncoder().encode(str); } function xlsxDosDateTime() { const d = new Date(); const dosTime = ((d.getHours() & 0x1F) << 11) | ((d.getMinutes() & 0x3F) << 5) | ((d.getSeconds() >> 1) & 0x1F); const dosDate = (((d.getFullYear() - 1980) & 0x7F) << 9) | (((d.getMonth() + 1) & 0xF) << 5) | (d.getDate() & 0x1F); return { dosTime, dosDate }; } // files: array of { name: string, content: Uint8Array } function xlsxCreateZip(files) { const { dosTime, dosDate } = xlsxDosDateTime(); const localParts = []; const centralParts = []; let offset = 0; for (const file of files) { const nameBytes = xlsxStrToBytes(file.name); const data = file.content; const crc = xlsxCrc32(data); const size = data.length; const localHeader = new Uint8Array(30 + nameBytes.length); const lv = new DataView(localHeader.buffer); lv.setUint32(0, 0x04034b50, true); lv.setUint16(4, 20, true); lv.setUint16(6, 0, true); lv.setUint16(8, 0, true); lv.setUint16(10, dosTime, true); lv.setUint16(12, dosDate, true); lv.setUint32(14, crc, true); lv.setUint32(18, size, true); lv.setUint32(22, size, true); lv.setUint16(26, nameBytes.length, true); lv.setUint16(28, 0, true); localHeader.set(nameBytes, 30); localParts.push(localHeader, data); const centralHeader = new Uint8Array(46 + nameBytes.length); const cv = new DataView(centralHeader.buffer); cv.setUint32(0, 0x02014b50, true); cv.setUint16(4, 20, true); cv.setUint16(6, 20, true); cv.setUint16(8, 0, true); cv.setUint16(10, 0, true); cv.setUint16(12, dosTime, true); cv.setUint16(14, dosDate, true); cv.setUint32(16, crc, true); cv.setUint32(20, size, true); cv.setUint32(24, size, true); cv.setUint16(28, nameBytes.length, true); cv.setUint16(30, 0, true); cv.setUint16(32, 0, true); cv.setUint16(34, 0, true); cv.setUint16(36, 0, true); cv.setUint32(38, 0, true); cv.setUint32(42, offset, true); centralHeader.set(nameBytes, 46); centralParts.push(centralHeader); offset += localHeader.length + data.length; } const centralDirOffset = offset; let centralDirSize = 0; for (const part of centralParts) centralDirSize += part.length; const eocd = new Uint8Array(22); const ev = new DataView(eocd.buffer); ev.setUint32(0, 0x06054b50, true); ev.setUint16(4, 0, true); ev.setUint16(6, 0, true); ev.setUint16(8, files.length, true); ev.setUint16(10, files.length, true); ev.setUint32(12, centralDirSize, true); ev.setUint32(16, centralDirOffset, true); ev.setUint16(20, 0, true); const allParts = [...localParts, ...centralParts, eocd]; let totalLen = 0; for (const p of allParts) totalLen += p.length; const result = new Uint8Array(totalLen); let pos = 0; for (const p of allParts) { result.set(p, pos); pos += p.length; } return result; } function xlsxXmlEscape(v) { return String(v == null ? "" : v) .replace(/&/g, "&").replace(//g, ">") .replace(/"/g, """).replace(/'/g, "'"); } function xlsxColLetter(n) { let s = ""; n = n + 1; while (n > 0) { const rem = (n - 1) % 26; s = String.fromCharCode(65 + rem) + s; n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26); } return s; } function xlsxCellXml(colIdx, rowIdx, value, styleId, isNumber) { const ref = xlsxColLetter(colIdx) + (rowIdx + 1); const sAttr = styleId ? ` s="${styleId}"` : ""; if (isNumber && value !== "" && value != null && Number.isFinite(Number(value))) { return `${Number(value)}`; } const text = xlsxXmlEscape(value); return `${text}`; } function buildXlsxBytes(title, subtitle, headers, rows, opts = {}) { const { numericCols = [], moneyCols = [], totalRow = null } = opts; const ncols = headers.length; const stylesXml = ` `; let rowXml = []; let r = 0; rowXml.push(`${xlsxCellXml(0, r, title, 1, false)}`); r++; rowXml.push(`${xlsxCellXml(0, r, subtitle || "", 2, false)}`); r++; rowXml.push(``); r++; { const cells = headers.map((h, i) => xlsxCellXml(i, r, h, 3, false)).join(""); rowXml.push(`${cells}`); r++; } for (const dataRow of rows) { const cells = dataRow.map((val, i) => { const isMoney = moneyCols.includes(i); const isNum = numericCols.includes(i) || isMoney; const styleId = isMoney ? 6 : (isNum ? 5 : 4); return xlsxCellXml(i, r, val, styleId, isNum); }).join(""); rowXml.push(`${cells}`); r++; } if (totalRow) { const cells = totalRow.map((val, i) => { const isMoney = moneyCols.includes(i); const isNum = numericCols.includes(i) || isMoney; const styleId = isMoney ? 8 : 7; return xlsxCellXml(i, r, val, styleId, isNum && val !== ""); }).join(""); rowXml.push(`${cells}`); r++; } const colWidths = headers.map((h, i) => { const isNum = numericCols.includes(i) || moneyCols.includes(i); return Math.max(String(h).length + 4, isNum ? 12 : 14); }); const colsXml = colWidths.map((w, i) => `` ).join(""); const sheetXml = ` ${colsXml} ${rowXml.join("")} `; const workbookXml = ` `; const workbookRels = ` `; const rootRels = ` `; const contentTypes = ` `; const coreXml = ` ${xlsxXmlEscape(title)} DurianPMS `; const enc = (s) => new TextEncoder().encode(s); const files = [ { name: "[Content_Types].xml", content: enc(contentTypes) }, { name: "_rels/.rels", content: enc(rootRels) }, { name: "docProps/core.xml", content: enc(coreXml) }, { name: "xl/workbook.xml", content: enc(workbookXml) }, { name: "xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels", content: enc(workbookRels) }, { name: "xl/styles.xml", content: enc(stylesXml) }, { name: "xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml", content: enc(sheetXml) }, ]; return xlsxCreateZip(files); } // ── exportHTMLReport: opens a formatted HTML report in a new browser tab ── function exportHTMLReport({ title, subtitle, sections, filename="" }) { const farm = "Owl & Arbor Orchard @ Lenggeng"; const now = new Date().toLocaleDateString("en-MY",{ weekday:"long", year:"numeric", month:"long", day:"numeric" }); const rm2 = v => Number(v||0).toLocaleString("en-MY",{minimumFractionDigits:2,maximumFractionDigits:2}); // Build HTML string const sectionHTML = sections.map(sec => { if (sec.type === "summary") { // Key-value summary rows const rows = sec.rows.map(([label, value, highlight]) => ` ${label} ${value} ` ).join(""); return `
${sec.title ? `

${sec.title}

` : ""} ${rows}
`; } if (sec.type === "table") { const headers = sec.headers.map(h => `${h.label}` ).join(""); const rows = sec.rows.map((row, ri) => { const cells = row.map((cell, ci) => `${cell??""}` ).join(""); return `${cells}`; }).join(""); const totalRow = sec.totalRow ? ` ${sec.totalRow.map((cell,ci) => `${cell??""}` ).join("")} ` : ""; return `
${sec.title ? `

${sec.title}

` : ""} ${sec.subtitle ? `

const deletePromises = []; for(const [collName, items] of colls) { const backupIds = new Set((items||[]).map(i=>i.id).filter(Boolean)); const snap = await window.fbGetDocs(col(collName)); if(snap) { snap.docs.forEach(doc => { if(!backupIds.has(doc.id)) deletePromises.push(window.fbDeleteDoc(doc.ref)); }); } } // Attendance extras if(d.attendance) { const backupDates = new Set(Object.keys(d.attendance)); const attSnap = await window.fbGetDocs(col("attendance")); if(attSnap) { attSnap.docs.forEach(doc => { if(!backupDates.has(doc.id)) deletePromises.push(window.fbDeleteDoc(doc.ref)); }); } } if(deletePromises.length > 0) await Promise.all(deletePromises); // Step 3: Update local React state immediately if(d.trees) setTrees(d.trees); if(d.harvest) setHarvest(d.harvest); if(d.sales) setSales(d.sales);
